📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期呼吸道合胞病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 在4,537名患者中,有1,394名感染了RSV,3,143名感染了流感A病毒。在进行微生物学检测的患者中,RSV感染患者中有187/611(30.6%)存在细菌共感染,流感A病毒感染患者中有298/947(31.5%)存在细菌共感染(p = 0.72)。在所有患者中,RSV感染患者中有187/1,394(13.4%)存在细菌共感染,流感A病毒感染患者中有298/3,143(9.5%)存在细菌共感染(p < 0.01)。在两个组中,最常见的病原体是流感嗜血杆菌,其次是肺炎链球菌、卡他莫拉菌和金黄色葡萄球菌。
  • 在MDV 队列研究中,事件后30天内门诊就诊次数平均从1.21次增加到2.07次,住院次数从0.07次增加到0.37次,导致显著增加的医疗费用¥196,231(p < 0.001)。随着时间推移,影响逐渐减弱,在事件后365天期间未检测到统计学显著性。RSV病例数量在研究期间有所增加,分别达到2018–2019年的38例和2019–2020年的36例。
  • 多变量逻辑回归表明,升高的 AAPR 与 G+ 感染的可能性独立且呈负相关(OR = 0.37;95% CI:0.15-0.89,最高与 RCS 分析显示没有非线性关联的证据(非线性 P = 0.456)。单独的 AAPR 显示出适度的判别性能(AUC = 0.599)。将 AAPR 添加到 CRP、WBC、 NEU 将 AUC 从 0.455 增加到 0.613;然而,差异并未达到统计学显着性的常规阈值(P = 0.051)。
  • 在19,021例ALRI住院病例中,有661例(3.5%)为hMPV病例,2397例(12.6%)为RSV病例。hMPV患者年龄较大(中位数为10个月 vs 8个月;P < 0.001),合并症更多(59% vs 47%;P < 0.001),且有更频繁的既往呼吸道住院史(42% vs 20%)。hMPV患者发热更常见(74% vs 65%;P < 0.001),且以局灶性肺炎为主,而毛细支气管炎则较少见。RSV需要更高级的呼吸支持。死亡率的发病率极低且相似。hMPV的高峰晚于RSV。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Among 4,537 patients, 1,394 had RSV infection and 3,143 had influenza A infection.
  • In the MDV cohort, the average number of outpatient visits increased from 1.21 to 2.07, and hospitalizations increased from 0.07 to 0.37 within 30 days post-event, resulting in significant additional medical costs of ¥196,231 (p < 0.001).
  • From 2021 to 2024, RSV had the highest incidence rate (63.2/100 person-years [PY]; 95% CI, 56.6-69.8), followed by SARS-CoV-2 (39.3/100 PY; 95% CI, 34.5-44) and influenza (35.7/100 PY; 95% CI, 29-42.3).
  • Multivariable logistic regression demonstrated that elevated AAPR was independently and inversely associated with the likelihood of G+ infection (OR = 0.37; 95% CI: 0.15–0.89 for the highest vs.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Public health research & practice  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-12-01  |  DOI: 10.1071/pu25136  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 36, 4, PU25136

2019-2023 年悉尼儿童医院网络收治的幼儿的增强型 呼吸道合胞病毒 监测。

Enhanced respiratory syncytial virus surveillance of young children admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network, 2019-2023.
作者： Gemma L Saravanos, Kathryn Meredith, Shirley Wong, Rebecca Burrell, Adam Bartlett, Nusrat Homaira, Philip N Britton, Rama Kandasamy
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在全面描述被送往悉尼儿童医院网络的呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)疾病患儿的临床特征、管理及预后,确定严重疾病的总体负担,并识别特定人群进入重症监护病房(ICU)的风险因素。
设计与对象
本研究是一项在澳大利亚新南威尔士州首府地区两家三级儿科医院进行的为期5年的多中心回顾性研究,是在政府资助的儿童RSV疫苗接种计划实施前开展的。参与者包括2019年1月1日至2023年12月31日期间因实验室确诊RSV而住院的<2岁儿童,包括所有ICU入院患儿以及非ICU入院患儿的随机样本。
核心方法
本研究考察了<2岁儿童RSV住院和ICU入院的特征及发生率。通过多变量逻辑回归分析确定了与ICU入院高风险相关的因素。
主要结果
5 年期间,4414 名儿童因实验室确诊的 RSV 住院,其中 11.3% (499/4414) 入住 ICU。与非 ICU 样本 (n = 1000;3.3 个月 vs 7.1 个月,P < 0.0001) 相比,ICU 队列研究 中的中位年龄 (n = 499) 更年轻。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为RSV疫苗接种计划预计会显著降低澳大利亚儿童在出生第一年内的住院负担。他们还指出,原住民儿童、早产儿以及患有心脏疾病的儿童面临最严重的疾病风险,可能从扩展的预防计划中受益。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为制定针对高风险群体(如原住民儿童、早产儿和患有心脏疾病的儿童)的预防计划提供依据,以减少RSV住院的负担。
局限与证据强度
由于研究是在政府资助的疫苗接种计划实施前进行的,因此可能限制其评估此类计划影响的能力。由于研究的回顾性设计以及潜在的未测量混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Enhanced respiratory syncytial virus surveillance of young children admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network, 2019-2023.

Authors: Gemma L Saravanos, Kathryn Meredith, Shirley Wong, Rebecca Burrell, Adam Bartlett, Nusrat Homaira, Philip N Britton, Rama Kandasamy
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: In this study, we aimed to comprehensively describe the clinical characteristics, management and outcomes of children with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network (SCHN), determine the full burden of severe disease and identify population-specific risk factors for intensive care unit (ICU) admission. STUDY TYPE: Multi-centre, retrospective cohort study conducted over 5 years. METHODS: The study was conducted at two tertiary paediatric hospitals in metropolitan New South Wales, Australia, before implementation of government-funded immunisation programs against RSV in children. Participants were children aged <2 years hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV from 1 January 2019 to 31 December 2023, comprising all ICU admitted, and a random sample of non-ICU admitted patients. The main outcome measures were characteristics and incidence rate of RSV hospitalisations and ICU admissions among children aged <2 years. RESULTS: Over a 5-year period, 4414 children were hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV, of whom 11.3% (499/4414) were admitted to ICU. The median age was younger in the ICU cohort (n = 499) compared with the non-ICU sample (n = 1000; 3.3 vs 7.1 months, P < 0.0001). Factors associated with higher odds of ICU admission in multivariate logistic regression were decreasing month of age (OR 1.10, 95% CI 1.06-1.11), prematurity (OR 2.17, 95% CI 1.61-2.91), Indigenous identity (OR 4.10, 95% CI 2.30-7.56) and cardiac disease (OR 3.39, 95% CI 2.06-5.70). The average hospitalisation incidence rate was 1615 per 100,000 (95% CI 1565-1666), with higher rates among children aged <1 year, 2141 per 100,000 (95% CI 2061-2224) compared with those aged 1-2 years, 1088 per 100,000 (95% CI 1030-1148). CONCLUSIONS: RSV immunisation programs are expected to substantially reduce the burden of hospitalisation among Australian children in their first year of life. Children who are Indigenous, were born prematurely or have heart disease are at greatest risk of severe disease and may benefit from extended preventative programs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to comprehensively describe the clinical characteristics, management and outcomes of children with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network, determine the full burden of severe disease and identify population-specific risk factors for intensive care unit (ICU) admission.
Design and population
The study was a multi-centre, retrospective cohort study conducted over 5 years at two tertiary paediatric hospitals in metropolitan New South Wales, Australia, before implementation of government-funded immunisation programs against RSV in children. Participants included children aged <2 years hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV from 1 January 2019 to 31 December 2023, comprising all ICU admitted and a random sample of non-ICU admitted patients.
Core methods
The study involved examining the characteristics and incidence rate of RSV hospitalisations and ICU admissions among children aged <2 years. Factors associated with higher odds of ICU admission were identified using multivariate logistic regression.
Main results
Over a 5-year period, 4414 children were hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV, of whom 11.3% (499/4414) were admitted to ICU. The median age was younger in the ICU cohort (n = 499) compared with the non-ICU sample (n = 1000; 3.3 vs 7.1 months, P < 0.0001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that RSV immunisation programs are expected to substantially reduce the burden of hospitalisation among Australian children in their first year of life. They also noted that children who are Indigenous, were born prematurely or have heart disease are at greatest risk of severe disease and may benefit from extended preventative programs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of targeted preventative programs for high-risk groups, such as Indigenous children, those born prematurely and those with cardiac disease, to reduce the burden of RSV hospitalisations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted before the implementation of government-funded immunisation programs, which may limit its ability to assess the impact of such programs. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the retrospective design and potential for unmeasured confounding.

后疫情时代的人类副流感病毒:与呼吸道合胞病毒在阿根廷的多中心比较研究,2022–2025

Human Metapneumovirus in the Post-pandemic Era:: A Multicenter Comparative Study With Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Argentina, 2022–2025
作者： Angela S. de Gentile, María Del Valle Juarez, María Florencia Lucion, Gabriela Gregorio, Oscar Lopez, María Natalia Pejito, Andrea Falaschi, Paula Y Zuzel, Silvina Lobertti, Andres Gioiosa
摘要中文翻译背景:人类副流感病毒(hMPV)自2001年被发现以来,已成为全球儿童急性下呼吸道感染(ALRI)的重要病因,通常被认为是仅次于呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)的第二常见病毒病因。然而,在疫情后时期,关于hMPV与RSV的比较数据在拉丁美洲仍较为有限,尤其是在引入母体RSV疫苗接种后。方法:我们于2022年至2025年在阿根廷的5家三级儿科医院开展了一项前瞻性多中心研究。纳入了经逆转录酶聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)确认的hMPV或RSV相关ALRI住院儿童。比较了流行病学特征、临床表现、呼吸支持需求、结局及季节性模式。使用多变量逻辑回归分析评估hMPV与RSV感染的独立预测因素。结果:在19,021例ALRI住院病例中,有661例(3.5%)为hMPV感染,2397例(12.6%)为RSV感染。hMPV感染患儿年龄较大(中位数10个月 vs 8个月;P < 0.001),合并症更多(59% vs 47%;P < 0.001),且有更多既往呼吸道住院史(42% vs. 发热在hMPV感染中更为常见(74% vs. 65%;P < 0.001),且以局灶性肺炎为主,而RSV则需要更高级的呼吸支持。hMPV的死亡率极低且与RSV相当。hMPV的独立预测因素包括年龄6-23个月和既往呼吸道住院史;与2022年相比,2023-2025年hMPV的风险较低。hMPV的高峰出现时间晚于RSV。结论:这种新兴的呼吸道病毒表现出独特的流行病学和临床特征,主要影响有呼吸道脆弱性的年长儿童。在RSV预防策略实施的时代,hMPV可能成为严重儿科ALRI残留负担的重要贡献者,凸显了开发预防干预措施的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人类副流感病毒(hMPV)是全球儿童急性下呼吸道感染(ALRI)的重要病因,而在后疫情时期,hMPV与呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)之间的比较数据在拉丁美洲仍较为有限,特别是在引入母体RSV免疫接种后。
设计与对象
在阿根廷的5家三级儿科医院,于2022年至2025年期间进行了一项前瞻性多中心监测研究,纳入了因逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)确认的hMPV或RSV相关ALRI而住院的儿童。
核心方法
比较了流行病学特征、临床表现、呼吸支持需求、结局和季节性模式,并使用多变量逻辑回归分析了hMPV与RSV感染的独立预测因素。
主要结果
在19,021例ALRI住院病例中,有661例(3.5%)为hMPV病例,2397例(12.6%)为RSV病例。hMPV患者年龄较大(中位数为10个月 vs 8个月;P < 0.001),合并症更多(59% vs 47%;P < 0.001),且有更频繁的既往呼吸道住院史(42% vs 20%)。hMPV患者发热更常见(74% vs 65%;P < 0.001),且以局灶性肺炎为主,而毛细支气管炎则较少见。RSV需要更高级的呼吸支持。死亡率的发病率极低且相似。hMPV的高峰晚于RSV。
解释与创新
本研究强调,hMPV表现出独特的流行病学和临床特征,主要影响有呼吸道脆弱性的较大儿童。
科研与公卫意义
在RSV预防策略的时代,hMPV可能成为严重儿科ALRI残留负担的重要贡献者,凸显了开发预防干预措施的必要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其观察性设计和潜在的混杂因素,但由于样本量大且分析全面,证据强度较强。

Human Metapneumovirus in the Post-pandemic Era:: A Multicenter Comparative Study With Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Argentina, 2022–2025

Authors: Angela S. de Gentile, María Del Valle Juarez, María Florencia Lucion, Gabriela Gregorio, Oscar Lopez, María Natalia Pejito, Andrea Falaschi, Paula Y Zuzel, Silvina Lobertti, Andres Gioiosa
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Human metapneumovirus (hMPV), identified in 2001, is an important cause of acute lower respiratory infections (ALRI) in children worldwide and is generally considered the second most frequent viral cause after respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Comparative data between hMPV and RSV in the post-pandemic period remain scarce in Latin America, particularly after the introduction of maternal RSV immunization. METHODS: We conducted a prospective multicenter surveillance study in 5 tertiary pediatric hospitals in Argentina between 2022 and 2025. Children hospitalized with reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction-confirmed hMPV or RSV-associated ALRI were included. Epidemiologic characteristics, clinical presentation, respiratory support requirements, outcomes and seasonal patterns were compared. Independent predictors of hMPV versus RSV infection were evaluated using multivariable logistic regression. RESULTS: Among 19,021 ALRI hospitalizations, 661 (3.5%) were hMPV and 2397 (12.6%) were RSV cases. hMPV patients were older (median 10 vs 8 months; P < 0.001), had more comorbidities (59% vs. 47%; P < 0.001) and had prior respiratory hospitalizations (42% vs. Fever was more frequent in hMPV (74% vs. 65%; P < 0.001), and focal pneumonia predominated over bronchiolitis. RSV required more advanced respiratory support. Mortality was extremely low and comparable. Independent predictors of hMPV included age 6-23 months and prior respiratory hospitalizations; hMPV risk was lower in 2023-2025 versus 2022. hMPV peaked later than RSV. CONCLUSIONS: This emerging respiratory virus demonstrates distinct epidemiologic and clinical characteristics, predominantly affecting older children with respiratory vulnerability. In the era of RSV prevention strategies, hMPV may represent an increasingly important contributor to the residual burden of severe pediatric ALRI, highlighting the need for development of preventive interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human metapneumovirus (hMPV) is an important cause of acute lower respiratory infections (ALRI) in children worldwide, and comparative data between hMPV and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in the post-pandemic period remain scarce in Latin America, particularly after the introduction of maternal RSV immunization.
Design and population
A prospective multicenter surveillance study was conducted in 5 tertiary pediatric hospitals in Argentina between 2022 and 2025, including children hospitalized with reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction-confirmed hMPV or RSV-associated ALRI.
Core methods
Epidemiologic characteristics, clinical presentation, respiratory support requirements, outcomes and seasonal patterns were compared, and independent predictors of hMPV versus RSV infection were evaluated using multivariable logistic regression.
Main results
Among 19,021 ALRI hospitalizations, 661 (3.5%) were hMPV and 2397 (12.6%) were RSV cases. hMPV patients were older (median 10 vs 8 months; P < 0.001), had more comorbidities (59% vs 47%; P < 0.001) and had prior respiratory hospitalizations (42% vs 20%). Fever was more frequent in hMPV (74% vs 65%; P < 0.001), and focal pneumonia predominated over bronchiolitis. RSV required more advanced respiratory support. Mortality was extremely low and comparable. hMPV peaked later than RSV.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights that hMPV demonstrates distinct epidemiologic and clinical characteristics, predominantly affecting older children with respiratory vulnerability.
Scientific and public-health significance
In the era of RSV prevention strategies, hMPV may represent an increasingly important contributor to the residual burden of severe pediatric ALRI, highlighting the need for development of preventive interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and comprehensive analysis.

Journal: Journal of Virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-06  |  DOI: 10.1128/jvi.00863-26  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, e0086326
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

发病率与流感、COVID-19及呼吸道合胞病毒相关急性呼吸道疾病的危险因素:一项出生队列研究研究

Incidence and risk factors of acute respiratory illness associated with influenza, COVID-19, and respiratory syncytial virus within a birth cohort study
作者： Bryden Bird, Louise Thorn, Nayyereh Aminisani, Tim Wood, Ruth Seeds, Tineke Jennings, Lauren Jelley, Gail O'Leary, Michelle N. D. Balm, Tony Dowell
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流感病毒、严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2(SARS-CoV-2)以及呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)是婴幼儿急性呼吸道感染(ARIs)的主要原因,但目前的理解主要依赖于医院为基础的监测,低估了更广泛的社区负担。
设计与对象
本研究分析了2020-2024年期间在新西兰进行的南方半球流感和疫苗有效性研究及监测 3(SHIVERS-III)出生队列研究中注册的843名0至4岁参与者的数据。
核心方法
从有症状的参与者中采集鼻拭子,并通过RT-PCR检测病毒RNA。在监测期间,估计了发病率率(IRs)和发病率率比(IRRs),并根据年龄、性别、种族、社会经济状况和流感疫苗接种状态进行了调整。
主要结果
From 2021 to 2024, RSV had the highest incidence rate (63.2/100 person-years [PY]; 95% CI, 56.6-69.8), followed by SARS-CoV-2 (39.3/100 PY; 95% CI, 34.5-44) and influenza (35.7/100 PY; 95% CI, 29-42.3).
解释与创新
年龄影响感染风险:6个月以下的婴儿流感和RSV的发病率最低,这可能反映了母源性免疫和有限的暴露。6个月后风险增加,RSV在1岁时达到峰值,流感在2岁时达到峰值。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现加强了针对特定年龄的免疫接种策略的证据基础,并提高了对早期生命呼吸道病毒流行病学的理解。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告与研究设计或分析方法相关的具体局限性。

Incidence and risk factors of acute respiratory illness associated with influenza, COVID-19, and respiratory syncytial virus within a birth cohort study

Authors: Bryden Bird, Louise Thorn, Nayyereh Aminisani, Tim Wood, Ruth Seeds, Tineke Jennings, Lauren Jelley, Gail O'Leary, Michelle N. D. Balm, Tony Dowell
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: Influenza virus, severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) are leading causes of acute respiratory infections (ARIs). New Zealand's coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) public health measures disrupted the circulation of these viruses in 2020-2021, creating a rare opportunity to explore the impact of potential immunity gaps on age-specific infection risk. We analyzed data from 843 participants aged between 0 and 4 years enrolled in the Southern Hemisphere Influenza and Vaccine Effectiveness Research and Surveillance 3 (SHIVERS-III) birth cohort, a longitudinal community-based study in New Zealand, during 2020-2024. During the respiratory season, weekly surveillance captured ARI symptoms and severity. Nasal swabs were collected from symptomatic participants and tested for viral RNA by RT-PCR. Incidence rates (IRs) and incidence rate ratios (IRRs) were estimated for the surveillance period and adjusted for age, sex, ethnic group, socioeconomic status, and influenza vaccination status. No influenza or SARS-CoV-2 infections were detected in 2020 and 2021. From 2021 to 2024, RSV had the highest IR during the surveillance period (63.2/100 person-years [PY]; 95% CI, 56.6-69.8), followed by SARS-CoV-2 (39.3/100 PY; 95% CI, 34.5-44) and influenza (35.7/100 PY; 95% CI, 29-42.3). Age influenced infection risk: infants <6 months had the lowest incidence of influenza and RSV, potentially reflecting maternally derived immunity and limited exposure. Risk increased after 6 months of age, peaking at age 1 year for RSV and at age 2 years for influenza. Influenza vaccination lowered symptomatic influenza risk (IRR 0.66; 95% CI, 0.44-1.00). In the New Zealand community setting in the post-COVID-19 era, individual respiratory virus illness patterns differ in children aged 0-4. Influenza vaccine shows protection, and age influences virus-specific risk. IMPORTANCE: Influenza virus, severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) are major causes of acute respiratory illness in early childhood, yet current understanding is largely reliant on hospital-based surveillance that underestimates the broader community burden. This study addresses this limitation by providing comprehensive, community-based estimates of incidence, severity, and symptom profiles across these viruses within a single longitudinal birth cohort. By leveraging the unique epidemiological context created by pandemic-related disruptions, it offers important insights into how changes in exposure influence subsequent infection risk and virus circulation. The direct comparison of three major respiratory viruses within the same population highlights distinct age-related risk patterns and disease characteristics. In addition, our research provides real-world evidence of the reduced risk of influenza-associated acute respiratory illness among vaccinated children. These findings strengthen the evidence base for age-targeted immunization strategies and improve understanding of early-life respiratory virus epidemiology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza virus, severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) are leading causes of acute respiratory infections (ARIs) in early childhood, yet current understanding is largely reliant on hospital-based surveillance that underestimates the broader community burden.
Design and population
This study analyzed data from 843 participants aged between 0 and 4 years enrolled in the Southern Hemisphere Influenza and Vaccine Effectiveness Research and Surveillance 3 (SHIVERS-III) birth cohort, a longitudinal community-based study in New Zealand, during 2020-2024.
Core methods
Nasal swabs were collected from symptomatic participants and tested for viral RNA by RT-PCR. Incidence rates (IRs) and incidence rate ratios (IRRs) were estimated for the surveillance period and adjusted for age, sex, ethnic group, socioeconomic status, and influenza vaccination status.
Main results
From 2021 to 2024, RSV had the highest incidence rate (63.2/100 person-years [PY]; 95% CI, 56.6-69.8), followed by SARS-CoV-2 (39.3/100 PY; 95% CI, 34.5-44) and influenza (35.7/100 PY; 95% CI, 29-42.3).
Interpretation and novelty
Age influenced infection risk: infants <6 months had the lowest incidence of influenza and RSV, potentially reflecting maternally derived immunity and limited exposure. Risk increased after 6 months of age, peaking at age 1 year for RSV and at age 2 years for influenza.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings strengthen the evidence base for age-targeted immunization strategies and improve understanding of early-life respiratory virus epidemiology.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations specific to the study design or analysis methods.

东部中国2020-2024年间人偏肺病毒相关急性呼吸道感染住院率与呼吸道合胞病毒及流感的比较:一项时间序列分析。

Human metapneumovirus-associated ARI hospitalisation rates in comparison to respiratory syncytial virus and influenza in eastern China, 2020-2024: a time-series analysis.
作者： Miaojia Lu, Mengling Qiao, Shuting Chen, Jiayi Zhu, You Li, Xin Wang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在估测东部中国人偏肺病毒(hMPV)相关急性呼吸道感染(ARI)的住院率,并在2020-2024年间直接与呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)和流感进行比较。
设计与对象
利用一个大型区域医疗数据库,进行了时间序列分析,考察了2020年至2024年间东部中国按年龄组和区域划分的每周ARI住院率和hMPV、RSV、流感的病毒检测阳性情况。
核心方法
使用带有自然三次样条曲线和时间偏移量的负二项回归模型分析病毒活动,排除了患有 COVID-19 的患者,并调整了 COVID-19 波浪的影响。
主要结果
hMPV与100,792(5.35%)的ARI住院率相关,RSV与266,826(14.17%)相关,流感与409,599(21.76%)相关。RSV住院率在2021/2022年率先反弹,随后是hMPV在2022/2023年,而流感住院率在研究期间每年增加;同时,hMPV和流感相关的住院率向5-19岁年龄组转移,而RSV相关的住院率则向≥5岁年龄组转移。
解释与创新
这些发现突出了hMPV和RSV住院率在年龄相关的不同模式,其中hMPV在0-5个月和6-11个月的婴儿中显示出更高的负担,而RSV和流感则在更小的年龄组中具有更高的住院率。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为针对监测、诊断以及干预策略提供了关键的见解,特别是针对婴儿和幼儿,以减轻hMPV和RSV感染的负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于区域医疗数据库,并排除了患有 COVID-19 的患者,这可能会限制其普遍适用性。由于是时间序列分析的观察性研究,证据等级为中等。

Human metapneumovirus-associated ARI hospitalisation rates in comparison to respiratory syncytial virus and influenza in eastern China, 2020-2024: a time-series analysis.

Authors: Miaojia Lu, Mengling Qiao, Shuting Chen, Jiayi Zhu, You Li, Xin Wang
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: We estimated human metapneumovirus (hMPV)-associated acute respiratory infection (ARI) hospitalisations in eastern China, and provided a direct comparison with RSV and influenza during 2020-2024. METHODS: Leveraging a large regional medical database, we analysed weekly ARI hospitalisations and viral test-positives (hMPV, RSV and influenza) by age group and region, using negative binomial regression models with natural cubic splines, time-offsets for viral activity, excluding COVID-19 patients and adjusting for COVID-19 waves. RESULTS: hMPV, RSV, and influenza were associated with 100,792 (5.35%), 266,826 (14.17%), and 409,599 (21.76%) ARI hospitalisations, respectively. RSV hospitalisations rebounded first in 2021/2022, followed by hMPV in 2022/2023, while influenza hospitalisations increased annually during the study period; meanwhile, hMPV- and influenza-associated hospitalisations shifted towards those aged 5-19 years, while RSV-associated hospitalisations shifted towards those aged ≥5 years. hMPV rates were highest among infants aged 0-5 months and 6-11 months (6.19 and 8.15 per 1,000 per year on average, respectively), followed by ages 12-23 months (18.54). RSV and influenza rates were highest in age groups within <1 year and 1-4 years, respectively. CONCLUSIONS: Infants aged <1 year are the primary group for preventing severe hMPV infections, with a substantial additional burden observed in children aged 1-4 years. These findings support hMPV vaccine development and virus-specific intervention strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to estimate the hospitalisation rates of human metapneumovirus (hMPV)-associated acute respiratory infections (ARI) in eastern China and compare them directly with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and influenza during 2020-2024.
Design and population
A time-series analysis was conducted using a large regional medical database, examining weekly ARI hospitalisations and viral test-positives for hMPV, RSV, and influenza by age group and region in eastern China from 2020 to 2024.
Core methods
Negative binomial regression models with natural cubic splines and time-offsets for viral activity were used, excluding patients with COVID-19 and adjusting for the impact of COVID-19 waves.
Main results
hMPV was associated with 100,792 (5.35%) ARI hospitalisations, RSV with 266,826 (14.17%), and influenza with 409,599 (21.76%). RSV hospitalisations rebounded first in 2021/2022, followed by hMPV in 2022/2023, while influenza hospitalisations increased annually. hMPV- and influenza-associated hospitalisations shifted towards those aged 5-19 years, while RSV-associated hospitalisations shifted towards those aged ≥5 years.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the distinct age-related patterns of hMPV and RSV hospitalisations, with hMPV showing a higher burden in infants aged 0-5 months and 6-11 months, and RSV and influenza having higher rates in younger age groups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide critical insights for targeted surveillance, diagnosis, and intervention strategies, particularly for infants and young children, to mitigate the burden of hMPV and RSV infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on a regional medical database and excludes patients with COVID-19, which may limit generalisability. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the time-series analysis.

呼吸道合胞病毒:其在婴儿初级保健中的临床和经济影响反思。作者回复。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus: reflections on its clinical and economic impact in infant primary care. Authors reply.
作者： María Garcés Sánchez, Ana Cubero Santos, Juan Antonio García Sánchez, César García Vera, Red de Investigación en Pediatría de Atención Primaria (PAPenRED), Garcés Sánchez M, Cubero Santos A, García Sánchez JA, García Vera C, Red de Investigación en Pediatría de Atención Primaria (PAPenRED).
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述在真实世界初级保健环境中,微生物学确诊的呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)感染的临床和经济负担,以了解RSV对医疗系统和儿科人群的影响。
设计与对象
在初级保健环境中开展了一项前瞻性队列研究研究,入组时间从2021年11月至2024年1月。暴露变量为RSV感染的微生物学确认,研究聚焦于5岁以下儿童。
核心方法
本研究涉及从初级保健记录中收集临床和经济数据,其中RSV感染的微生物学确认作为暴露变量。入组时间的选择是为了避开SARS-CoV-2病例的高峰期,分析包括对RSV与SARS-CoV-2共同检测情况的考察。
主要结果
仅有12份样本(2.32%)检测出SARS-CoV-2阳性,其中RSV/SARS-CoV-2共同检测的有3例(0.76%)。本研究突出了RSV相关病例中支气管扩张剂的过度使用。
解释与创新
作者承认SARS-CoV-2大流行对RSV 流行病学的影响,并指出本研究的重点是描述RSV的负担,而非评估预后因素。他们强调在分析结果时应考虑大流行后的背景情况。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为了解RSV在初级保健中的临床和经济影响提供了见解,有助于为儿科呼吸道感染的医疗资源分配和卫生规划提供依据。
局限与证据强度
由于缺乏SARS-CoV-2感染史的统一记录以及大流行后背景对患者招募的影响,本研究的设计存在局限性。这些局限性导致证据强度中等。

Authors: María Garcés Sánchez, Ana Cubero Santos, Juan Antonio García Sánchez, César García Vera, Red de Investigación en Pediatría de Atención Primaria (PAPenRED), Garcés Sánchez M, Cubero Santos A, García Sánchez JA, García Vera C, Red de Investigación en Pediatría de Atención Primaria (PAPenRED).
Original abstract (English)
After reading the letter 1 in response to our article, ''Clinical and economic impact on the primary care system of respiratory syncytial virus infection in children under 5 years'', 2 we would like to make the following comments:We agree that the SARS-CoV-2 pandemic had a substantial impact on the epidemiology of pediatric respiratory infections 3 and that previous infection by this virus could act as a confounder in some studies.However, the objective of our study was not to assess prognostic factors or determinants of severity, but to describe the clinical and economic burden associated with microbiologically confirmed episodes of infection by respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in real-world everyday clinical practice at the primary care (PC) level.We therefore designed a prospective cohort study in which the exposure variable was microbiological confirmation of RSV infection.The history of infection by SARS-CoV-2 was not uniformly documented in all participants, and, furthermore, more than half of COVID-19 cases in this age group are asymptomatic, 4,5 which precludes reliable classification.The enrollment period (November 2021 to January 2024) coincided with an epidemiological transition period after the pandemic, a circumstance that we addressed in the discussion section of the article as a factor that even affected patient recruitment.We deliberately avoided periods during which COVID-19 cases peaked, which was corroborated by the finding (not published in the original article) that only 12 samples (2.32%) were positive for SARS-CoV-2, with codetection of RSV/SARS-CoV-2 in 3 (0.76%).We have nothing to add to the comments regarding the excessive use of bronchodilators revealed by our study, as
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to describe the clinical and economic burden of microbiologically confirmed respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infections in real-world primary care settings, addressing the need to understand the impact of RSV on healthcare systems and pediatric populations.
Design and population
A prospective cohort study was conducted in primary care settings, enrolling participants from November 2021 to January 2024. The exposure variable was microbiological confirmation of RSV infection, and the study focused on children under 5 years of age.
Core methods
The study involved the collection of clinical and economic data from primary care records, with microbiological confirmation of RSV infection serving as the exposure variable. The enrollment period was chosen to avoid peak periods of SARS-CoV-2 cases, and the analysis included the examination of codetection with SARS-CoV-2.
Main results
Only 12 samples (2.32%) were positive for SARS-CoV-2, with codetection of RSV/SARS-CoV-2 in 3 (0.76%) cases. The study highlighted the excessive use of bronchodilators in RSV-related cases.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors acknowledge the impact of the SARS-CoV-2 pandemic on RSV epidemiology and note that the study's focus was on describing the burden of RSV rather than assessing prognostic factors. They emphasize the importance of considering the post-pandemic context in their findings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the clinical and economic impact of RSV in primary care, which can inform resource allocation and healthcare planning for pediatric respiratory infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design was limited by the lack of uniform documentation of SARS-CoV-2 infection history and the post-pandemic context affecting patient recruitment. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

压力之下:从澳大利亚悉尼快速实施 呼吸道合胞病毒 孕产妇 疫苗 和婴儿 尼塞韦单抗 中吸取的经验教训。

Under pressure: lessons learnt from rapid implementation of the respiratory syncytial virus maternal vaccine and infant nirsevimab in Sydney, Australia.
作者： Isabella Townshend, Kate Griew, Rosemary Burke, Vendula Blaya-Novakova, Rebekah Dashwood, Sarah Everett, Leena Gupta
摘要中文翻译目标:分享悉尼地方卫生区 (SLHD) 推出 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 预防产品的经验教训,并提供两家妇产医院母亲和婴儿的免疫接种数据。服务类型:推出 RSV 母体 疫苗 和用于新生儿的可注射长效单克隆抗体 尼塞韦单抗,以保护他们免受 RSV 感染和住院治疗。方法:以计数和比例的形式对两家 SLHD 妇产医院的 RSV 免疫状况电子病历中的去识别化汇总数据进行描述性分析。实施研究综合框架被用作反思推出过程的镜头。结果:两家 SLHD 医院分娩妇女的 RSV 疫苗接种 覆盖率从推出第一个月(2025 年 2 月)的 4% 提高到 2025 年 6 月至 9 月的 70-74%。随着产前覆盖率的提高,需要 尼塞韦单抗 的活产婴儿数量减少。在符合条件的新生儿中,36%的家庭拒绝了尼塞韦单抗,但没有记录拒绝的原因。随着推广的进展,RSV 免疫状态的记录得到改善。经验教训:多学科领导者和临床医生的参与对于协调应对和计划成功推出至关重要。利用现有的教育结构、员工会议和使用在线预录音可以优化临床医生教育。新计划需要时间才能融入常规临床实践,而涉及为不同群体提供多种免疫产品的计划需要明确的信息传递和系统保障措施以防止错误。将免疫接种作为标准做法纳入医疗记录工作流程可以支持计划的采用和采用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在分享快速实施 RSV 预防产品的经验教训,并提供两家妇产医院母亲和婴儿的免疫接种数据。该研究解决了对有效策略的需求,以改善 RSV 疫苗 和 尼塞韦单抗 在临床环境中的采用。
设计与对象
本研究涉及在悉尼本地卫生区的两家 maternity 医院推广呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)母体疫苗 疫苗 和可注射单克隆抗体 尼塞韦单抗,考察了 2025 年 2 月至 9 月期间的疫苗接种率。
核心方法
从电子病历中提取的匿名汇总数据被描述性分析为计数和比例。采用实施研究综合框架(Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research)评估推广过程。
主要结果
在两家医院分娩的女性中,RSV 母体疫苗 疫苗接种 的接种率从推广首月的 4% 上升到 2025 年 6 月至 9 月期间的 70-74%。随着产前接种率的提高,需要 尼塞韦单抗 的活产婴儿数量减少。在符合条件的新生儿中,36% 的家庭拒绝接种 尼塞韦单抗,但拒绝的原因未被记录。在推广过程中,RSV 疫苗接种状态的记录有所改善。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明多学科协作和清晰信息传达在成功推广 RSV 预防计划中的重要性。该研究突出了在常规临床实践中整合多种疫苗产品的挑战和策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为未来 RSV 预防计划的制定提供参考,强调需要协调努力、医务人员教育和系统性保障措施以确保准确实施。它们还提供了改善 maternity 环境中疫苗接种率和记录的见解。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其依赖匿名汇总数据,且未记录 尼塞韦单抗 拒绝的原因。证据强度中等,因为它反映了实际实施中的挑战,但缺乏更深入的因果分析或对照组。

Under pressure: lessons learnt from rapid implementation of the respiratory syncytial virus maternal vaccine and infant nirsevimab in Sydney, Australia.

Authors: Isabella Townshend, Kate Griew, Rosemary Burke, Vendula Blaya-Novakova, Rebekah Dashwood, Sarah Everett, Leena Gupta
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To share lessons learnt from the Sydney Local Health District (SLHD) rollout of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prevention products, and present data on immunisation uptake for mothers and babies in two maternity hospitals. TYPE OF SERVICE: Rollout of the RSV maternal vaccine and the injectable, long-acting monoclonal antibody nirsevimab for newborn infants to protect them from RSV infection and hospitalisation. METHODS: Deidentified summary data from the electronic medical record on RSV immunisation status in two SLHD maternity hospitals were analysed descriptively as counts and proportions. The Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research was used as a lens to reflect on the rollout process. RESULTS: RSV vaccination coverage in women giving birth in two SLHD hospitals improved from 4% in the first month of rollout (February 2025) to a range of 70-74% from June to September 2025. As antenatal coverage improved, the number of live born babies requiring nirsevimab decreased. Among eligible newborns, 36% of families declined nirsevimab, with reason for refusal not recorded. Documentation of RSV immunisation status improved as the rollout progressed. LESSONS LEARNT: Engaging multidisciplinary leaders and clinicians is critical for a coordinated response and program rollout success. Leveraging existing education structures, staff meetings and using online prerecording can optimise clinician education. New programs take time to embed into routine clinical practice, and programs that involve delivering multiple immunisation products for distinct groups require clear messaging and systemic safeguards to prevent errors. Integrating immunisation into medical record workflows as standard practice can support program adoption and uptake.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to share lessons from the rapid implementation of RSV prevention products and present data on immunisation uptake for mothers and babies in two maternity hospitals. The research addresses the need for effective strategies to improve RSV vaccine and nirsevimab adoption in clinical settings.
Design and population
The study involved the rollout of the RSV maternal vaccine and the injectable monoclonal antibody nirsevimab in two maternity hospitals within the Sydney Local Health District. It examined immunisation uptake over a period from February to September 2025.
Core methods
Deidentified summary data from electronic medical records were analysed descriptively as counts and proportions. The Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research was used to evaluate the rollout process.
Main results
RSV vaccination coverage in women giving birth in the two hospitals increased from 4% in the first month of rollout to 70-74% from June to September 2025. The number of live born babies requiring nirsevimab decreased as antenatal coverage improved. Among eligible newborns, 36% of families declined nirsevimab, with reasons for refusal not recorded. Documentation of RSV immunisation status improved during the rollout.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the importance of multidisciplinary engagement and clear messaging in the successful rollout of RSV prevention programs. The study highlights the challenges and strategies for integrating multiple immunisation products into routine clinical practice.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of future RSV prevention programs by emphasizing the need for coordinated efforts, clinician education, and systematic safeguards to ensure accurate implementation. They also provide insights into improving immunisation uptake and documentation in maternity settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on deidentified summary data and the absence of recorded reasons for nirsevimab refusal. The evidence is moderate, as it reflects real-world implementation challenges but lacks deeper causal analysis or control groups.

呼吸道合胞病毒和流感A病毒感染住院患者中的细菌共感染:一项回顾性观察性研究

Bacterial co-infection in patients hospitalized with respiratory syncytial virus and influenza a infection: a retrospective observational cohort study
作者： Christoffer Vigen, Bo Langhoff Hønge, Jacob Dvinge Redder, Thomas Greve, Lars Skov Dalgaard, Anita Rath Sørensen, Lotte Ebdrup, Rajesh Mohey, Britta Tarp, Lars Jørgen Østergaard
摘要中文翻译目的:比较呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)或流感A病毒感染住院成人患者中细菌共感染的流行情况、微生物学特征和临床影响。方法:我们对2015年至2023年间中央丹麦地区实验室确诊的RSV或流感A病毒感染住院成人患者进行了回顾性观察性研究。通过细菌培养和尿液抗原检测确定细菌共感染。使用泊松回归估计细菌共感染的风险比,并使用Cox比例风险回归评估细菌共感染与不良临床结局之间的关联。结果:在4,537名患者中,有1,394名感染了RSV,3,143名感染了流感A病毒。在进行微生物学检测的患者中,RSV感染患者中有187/611(30.6%)存在细菌共感染,流感A病毒感染患者中有298/947(31.5%)存在细菌共感染(p = 0.72)。在所有患者中,RSV感染患者中有187/1,394(13.4%)存在细菌共感染,流感A病毒感染患者中有298/3,143(9.5%)存在细菌共感染(p < 0.01)。在两个组中,最常见的病原体是流感嗜血杆菌,其次是肺炎链球菌、卡他莫拉菌和金黄色葡萄球菌。细菌共感染与流感A病毒感染患者的ICU入院和死亡率增加相关,而在RSV感染患者中估计值较低。结论:在进行微生物学检测的患者中,RSV和流感A病毒感染住院患者中细菌共感染的发生率相似。在两种病毒组中,最常见的分离细菌是携带β-内酰胺酶基因的细菌种类。这些发现支持在怀疑RSV和流感A病毒感染患者存在细菌共感染时,应考虑调整经验性抗生素治疗以覆盖携带β-内酰胺酶基因的病原体。细菌共感染与流感A病毒感染患者的临床结局较差相关,而与RSV感染患者的关联较弱。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在比较呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)或流感A病毒感染住院成人患者中细菌共感染的流行情况、微生物学特征和临床影响。
设计与对象
本研究是一项在中央丹麦地区2015年至2023年间实验室确诊的RSV或流感A病毒感染住院成人患者中进行的回顾性观察性研究。
核心方法
通过细菌培养和尿液抗原检测确定细菌共感染。使用泊松回归估计细菌共感染的风险比,并使用Cox比例风险回归评估细菌共感染与不良临床结局之间的关联。
主要结果
在4,537名患者中,有1,394名感染了RSV,3,143名感染了流感A病毒。在进行微生物学检测的患者中,RSV感染患者中有187/611(30.6%)存在细菌共感染,流感A病毒感染患者中有298/947(31.5%)存在细菌共感染(p = 0.72)。在所有患者中,RSV感染患者中有187/1,394(13.4%)存在细菌共感染,流感A病毒感染患者中有298/3,143(9.5%)存在细菌共感染(p < 0.01)。在两个组中,最常见的病原体是流感嗜血杆菌,其次是肺炎链球菌、卡他莫拉菌和金黄色葡萄球菌。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,RSV和流感A病毒感染住院患者中细菌共感染的发生率相似,且在两种病毒组中,最常见的分离细菌是携带β-内酰胺酶基因的细菌种类。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明,当怀疑RSV和流感A病毒感染患者存在细菌共感染时,经验性抗生素治疗应考虑覆盖携带β-内酰胺酶基因的病原体。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其回顾性设计和可能的选择偏倚,但由于样本量大且使用统计方法评估关联,证据是强有力的。

Bacterial co-infection in patients hospitalized with respiratory syncytial virus and influenza a infection: a retrospective observational cohort study

Authors: Christoffer Vigen, Bo Langhoff Hønge, Jacob Dvinge Redder, Thomas Greve, Lars Skov Dalgaard, Anita Rath Sørensen, Lotte Ebdrup, Rajesh Mohey, Britta Tarp, Lars Jørgen Østergaard
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: To compare the prevalence, microbiological characteristics, and clinical impact of bacterial co-infection in adults hospitalized with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) or influenza A infection. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective cohort study of adults hospitalized with laboratory-confirmed RSV or influenza A infection in Central Denmark. Bacterial co-infection was identified using bacterial culturing and urinary antigen testing. Poisson regression was used to estimate risk ratios for bacterial co-infection, and Cox proportional hazards regression was used to evaluate associations between bacterial co-infection and adverse clinical outcomes. RESULTS: Among 4,537 patients, 1,394 had RSV infection and 3,143 had influenza A infection. Among patients undergoing microbiological testing, bacterial co-infection was identified in 187/611 (30.6%) RSV-infected patients and 298/947 (31.5%) influenza A-infected patients (p = 0.72). Among all patients bacterial co-infection was identified in 187/1,394 (13.4%) RSV infected patients and 298/3,143 (9.5%) influenza A infected patients (p < 0.01) Haemophilus influenzae was the most common pathogen in both groups, followed by Streptococcus pneumoniae, Moraxella catarrhalis and Staphylococcus aureus. Bacterial co-infection was associated with increased ICU admission and mortality among influenza A infected patients, whereas estimates were lower in RSV. CONCLUSIONS: Among patients undergoing microbiological testing, bacterial co-infection was identified at similar rates in patients hospitalized with RSV and influenza A infection. The most frequently isolated bacteria in both viral groups were bacterial species harboring genes for beta-lactamase production. These findings support consideration of tailoring the empirical antibiotic therapy to cover pathogens with beta-lactamase production when bacterial co-infection is suspected in RSV and influenza A infected patients. Bacterial co-infection was associated with poorer clinical outcomes in Influenza A than RSV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare the prevalence, microbiological characteristics, and clinical impact of bacterial co-infection in adults hospitalized with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) or influenza A infection.
Design and population
A retrospective observational cohort study was conducted on adults hospitalized with laboratory-confirmed RSV or influenza A infection in Central Denmark between 2015 and 2023.
Core methods
Bacterial co-infection was identified using bacterial culturing and urinary antigen testing. Poisson regression was used to estimate risk ratios for bacterial co-infection, and Cox proportional hazards regression was used to evaluate associations between bacterial co-infection and adverse clinical outcomes.
Main results
Among 4,537 patients, 1,394 had RSV infection and 3,143 had influenza A infection. Among patients undergoing microbiological testing, bacterial co-infection was identified in 187/611 (30.6%) RSV-infected patients and 298/947 (31.5%) influenza A-infected patients (p = 0.72). Among all patients, bacterial co-infection was identified in 187/1,394 (13.4%) RSV infected patients and 298/3,143 (9.5%) influenza A infected patients (p < 0.01). Haemophilus influenzae was the most common pathogen in both groups, followed by Streptococcus pneumoniae, Moraxella catarrhalis and Staphylococcus aureus.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that bacterial co-infection occurs at similar rates in patients hospitalized with RSV and influenza A infection, and that Haemophilus influenzae is the most frequently isolated bacteria in both groups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that empirical antibiotic therapy should consider covering pathogens with beta-lactamase production when bacterial co-infection is suspected in RSV and influenza A infected patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias, but the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and use of statistical methods to evaluate associations.

在呼吸道合胞病毒患者呼吸道样本中分离出曲霉菌种:一项11年回顾性单中心队列研究研究。

Aspergillus species isolation in respiratory samples from patients with respiratory syncytial virus: An 11-year retrospective single-center cohort study.
作者： Alfredo Maldonado-Barrueco, María Amoroto-Bengoetxea, Gema Lozano-Fernández, Nuria González-Rico, Inmaculada Quiles-Melero, Julio García-Rodríguez
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 是成人中重要的致病因素,但关于除流感相关肺曲霉病和 COVID-19 相关肺曲霉病以外的真菌合并感染的数据仍有限。
设计与对象
我们在西班牙马德里的Hospital Universitario La Paz医院进行了一项11年的回顾性研究,纳入了17例确诊呼吸道合胞病毒感染且呼吸道样本中分离出曲霉菌的成人患者。
核心方法
根据临床、影像学和微生物学发现,将病例分为可能的侵袭性曲霉病或定植。
主要结果
A total of 18 episodes were identified, with the species within the Aspergillus fumigatus complex being the most frequently isolated (78.9%), followed by Aspergillus terreus complex (10.5%), Aspergillus nidulans complex (5.3%), and Aspergillus niger complex (5.3). Most isolates were considered colonization (55.6%), while probable invasive aspergillosis accounted for 44.4% episodes, based on compatible clinical and radiological findings. Corticosteroid exposure was common with 82.4% receiving or starting a corticosteroid therapy during hospitalization, and antifungal therapy was administered in 44.4% of episodes, including voriconazole and liposomal amphotericin B. The median hospital stay was 16 days (IQR: 8-28). Mortality was 11.1%.
解释与创新
我们的研究为与呼吸道合胞病毒感染相关的曲霉菌分离提供了新的见解,并强调需要进一步的多中心研究以明确流行病学、风险因素和最佳管理策略。
科研与公卫意义
在呼吸道合胞病毒感染的成人患者中,曲霉菌的分离主要见于有基础肺部疾病或合并症的患者,其中定植比侵袭性曲霉病更为常见。
局限与证据强度
然而,在本队列研究中未观察到需要重症监护的严重侵袭性曲霉病病例。

Aspergillus species isolation in respiratory samples from patients with respiratory syncytial virus: An 11-year retrospective single-center cohort study.

Authors: Alfredo Maldonado-Barrueco, María Amoroto-Bengoetxea, Gema Lozano-Fernández, Nuria González-Rico, Inmaculada Quiles-Melero, Julio García-Rodríguez
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Respiratory syncytial virus is a significant cause of morbidity in adults, but data on fungal coinfections, beyond influenza-associated pulmonary aspergillosis and COVID-19-associated pulmonary aspergillosis, remain limited. AIMS: To characterize the isolation of Aspergillus in respiratory samples from adult patients with confirmed respiratory syncytial virus infection over an 11-year period, and to describe associated risk factors, microbiological findings, antifungal treatment, and clinical outcomes. METHODS: We conducted an 11-year retrospective study at Hospital Universitario La Paz (Madrid, Spain) including 17 adult patients with confirmed respiratory syncytial virus infection and Aspergillus isolation from respiratory samples. Episodes were classified as probable invasive aspergillosis or colonization according to clinical, radiological, and microbiological findings. RESULTS: A total of 18 episodes were identified, with the species within the Aspergillus fumigatus complex being the most frequently isolated (78.9%), followed by Aspergillus terreus complex (10.5%), Aspergillus nidulans complex (5.3%), and Aspergillus niger complex (5.3). Most isolates were considered colonization (55.6%), while probable invasive aspergillosis accounted for 44.4% episodes, based on compatible clinical and radiological findings. Corticosteroid exposure was common with 82.4% receiving or starting a corticosteroid therapy during hospitalization, and antifungal therapy was administered in 44.4% of episodes, including voriconazole and liposomal amphotericin B. The median hospital stay was 16 days (IQR: 8-28). Mortality was 11.1%. CONCLUSIONS: Aspergillus isolation in adults with respiratory syncytial virus infection was observed mainly in patients with underlying pulmonary disease or comorbidities, with colonization being more frequent than invasive aspergillosis. However, no episodes of severe invasive aspergillosis requiring intensive care were observed in this cohort. Our study provides novel insights into Aspergillus isolation associated with respiratory syncytial virus infection and emphasizes the need for further multicentric studies to define epidemiology, risk factors, and optimal management strategies.

针对呼吸道合胞病毒的结合抗体与年龄特异性中和抗体及住院模式在以色列的相关性

Binding antibodies against respiratory syncytial virus and association with age-specific neutralizing antibodies and hospitalization patterns in Israel
作者： Ravit Bassal, Lital Keinan-Boker, Menucha Jurkowicz, Nofar Atari, Tomer Sinai, Yaniv Lustig, Ital Nemet, Michal Mandelboim, Lital Keinan‐Boker
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒(呼吸道合胞病毒)对健康造成显著负担,尤其是在婴儿和老年人中,但描述年龄特异性抗体反应与住院模式的全面人口水平数据仍然稀缺。为了评估RSV在不同人口统计学和地理变量中的血清阳性率,评估年龄特异性结合和中和抗体反应,并描述RSV阳性住院病例的年龄分布,我们使用了一个独立的住院队列研究数据集;任何血清调查与住院队列研究之间的比较都是描述性的和假设生成的。进行了一项横断面血清学调查,共收集了1,396份血清样本,通过ELISA检测RSV结合抗体(值被定义为高阳性、临界或低阳性)。其中,对243份按年龄分层的血清样本进行了中和抗体评估。在2022–2025年冬季,于单一中心收集了1,232份RSV阳性住院患者的独立队列研究数据集,用于评估年龄特异性住院分布;这两个数据集在个体层面未进行关联。总体而言,RSV高血清阳性率为7.8%,其中0–12个月的婴儿显示出最低的血清流行率(0.0%),而13–59个月的儿童(15.4%)和≥60岁的成年人(12.9%)则显示出最高水平。RSV几何平均滴度(GMTs)在不同年龄组间存在显著差异,从婴儿期到成年期逐渐上升,并在≥60岁的人群中达到峰值(p < 0.0001)。在非以色列出生的个体中观察到更高的GMTs,且GMTs在不同地区间存在显著差异,南部地区最高,特拉维夫地区最低。在住院队列研究中,婴儿占RSV阳性住院病例的最大比例(35.3%),其次是≥60岁的成年人(25.4%)。这些年龄特异性模式是在独立数据集中报告的,任何对比都是描述性的。在243份按年龄分层的血清样本中进行的中和抗体分析显示,在婴儿和幼儿中结合抗体与中和抗体之间存在强相关性,但在老年人群中相关性较弱或中等。在抗体的数量和功能质量方面观察到了年龄特异性差异,并且存在显著的人口统计学差异。由于血清调查和住院队列研究是独立的,因此观察到的抗体模式与住院分布之间的符合或不符合仅是描述性的,不能建立因果关系。这些模式是否与RSV易感性或住院风险相关,需要在具有个体层面数据的前瞻性研究中进一步探讨。这些发现强调了针对高风险群体(如婴儿和老年人)制定特定疫苗接种和预防策略的必要性。

问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒(呼吸道合胞病毒)对健康造成显著负担,尤其是在婴儿和老年人中,但描述年龄特异性抗体反应与住院模式的全面人口水平数据仍然稀缺。

总体而言,RSV高血清阳性率为7.8%,其中0–12个月的婴儿显示出最低的血清流行率(0.0%),而13–59个月的儿童(15.4%)和≥60岁的成年人(12.9%)则显示出最高水平。RSV几何平均滴度(GMTs)在不同年龄组间存在显著差异,从婴儿期到成年期逐渐上升,并在≥60岁的人群中达到峰值(p < 0.0001)。

科研与公卫意义

局限与证据强度

Binding antibodies against respiratory syncytial virus and association with age-specific neutralizing antibodies and hospitalization patterns in Israel

Authors: Ravit Bassal, Lital Keinan-Boker, Menucha Jurkowicz, Nofar Atari, Tomer Sinai, Yaniv Lustig, Ital Nemet, Michal Mandelboim, Lital Keinan‐Boker
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) imposes a substantial health burden, particularly in infants and older adults, but comprehensive population-level data describing age-specific antibody responses alongside hospitalization patterns are scarce. To assess RSV seropositivity across demographic and geographic variables, to evaluate age-specific binding and neutralizing antibody responses, and to describe the age distribution of RSV-positive hospitalizations in an independent hospitalized cohort; any comparisons between the serosurvey and the hospitalized cohort are descriptive and hypothesis‐generating. A cross-sectional sero-survey of 1,396 sera samples was conducted to measure RSV binding antibodies using ELISA (values were defined as high-positive, borderline or low-positive). Of them, neutralizing antibodies were assessed in 243 age-stratified sera. An independent cohort of 1,232 RSV-positive hospitalized patients collected during the 2022–2025 winter seasons at a single center was used to evaluate age-specific hospitalization distributions; the two datasets were not linked at the individual level. Overall, the RSV high-seropositivity rate was 7.8%, with infants aged 0–12 months showing the lowest seroprevalence (0.0%) while children aged 13–59 months (15.4%) and adults aged ≥ 60 years (12.9%) showed the highest. RSV geometric mean titers (GMTs) differed significantly by age, increasing from infancy to adulthood and peaking in those aged ≥ 60 years ( p < 0.0001). Higher GMTs were observed among individuals born outside Israel and varied significantly by district, with the highest levels in the South and lowest in Tel Aviv. Among the hospitalized cohort, infants accounted for the largest proportion of RSV-positive hospitalizations (35.3%), followed by adults aged ≥ 60 years (25.4%). These age‐specific patterns are reported for independent datasets and any juxtaposition is descriptive. Neutralizing antibody analysis in 243 age-stratified sera demonstrated strong correlations between binding and neutralizing antibodies in infants and young children, but weak to moderate correlations in older age groups. Age-specific differences in both the quantity and functional quality of antibodies were observed, with marked demographic variation. Because the serosurvey and hospitalized cohort are independent, the observed concordance or discordance between antibody patterns and hospitalization distribution is descriptive and does not establish causality. Whether these patterns relate to RSV susceptibility or hospitalization risk warrants further investigation in prospective studies with individual-level data. These findings highlight the need for targeted vaccination and prevention strategies, particularly for high-risk groups such as infants and older adults.

Question and background
Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) imposes a substantial health burden, particularly in infants and older adults, but comprehensive population-level data describing age-specific antibody responses alongside hospitalization patterns are scarce.
Design and population
To assess RSV seropositivity across demographic and geographic variables, to evaluate age-specific binding and neutralizing antibody responses, and to describe the age distribution of RSV-positive hospitalizations in an independent hospitalized cohort; any comparisons between the serosurvey and the hospitalized cohort are descriptive and hypothesis-generating.
Core methods
A cross-sectional sero-survey of 1,396 sera samples was conducted to measure RSV binding antibodies using ELISA (values were defined as high-positive, borderline or low-positive). Of them, neutralizing antibodies were assessed in 243 age-stratified sera.
Main results
Overall, the RSV high-seropositivity rate was 7.8%, with infants aged 0–12 months showing the lowest seroprevalence (0.0%) while children aged 13–59 months (15.4%) and adults aged ≥ 60 years (12.9%) showed the highest. RSV geometric mean titers (GMTs) differed significantly by age, increasing from infancy to adulthood and peaking in those aged ≥ 60 years ( p < 0.0001).
Interpretation and novelty
Age-specific differences in both the quantity and functional quality of antibodies were observed, with marked demographic variation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the need for targeted vaccination and prevention strategies, particularly for high-risk groups such as infants and older adults.
Limitations and evidence strength
Because the serosurvey and hospitalized cohort are independent, the observed concordance or discordance between antibody patterns and hospitalization distribution is descriptive and does not establish causality.

婴儿非意外创伤评估中出现的结膜下出血和呼吸道合胞病毒感染:一种可能关联的病例报告

Subconjunctival Hemorrhage and Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection in an Infant Evaluated for Non-accidental Trauma: A Case Report of a Possible Association
作者： Kyle Hatley Koester, Kathryn Martin, Natalie C. Kerr, Lauren R. Burge
摘要中文翻译一名六周大的既往健康的婴儿被发现患有结膜下出血(SCH)和同时发生的呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)感染。由于婴儿的结膜下出血可能代表非意外创伤(NAT)的警示征象,因此进行了全面的多学科评估,未发现创伤性损伤或虐待证据。结膜下出血在一周内消退,患者在接受支持性治疗后无并发症康复。据我们所知,结膜下出血与RSV的关联尚未有报道,尽管RSV在婴儿中很常见。与结膜下出血与其他呼吸道感染的报道类似,炎症和压力相关的机制可能是解释这种关联的原因,尽管这项观察性病例无法确立因果关系。报告这种可能的关联可能有助于扩大婴儿RSV感染相关结膜下出血的鉴别诊断范围,同时强调对虐待进行彻底筛查的重要性。识别儿童伤害的医学原因与准确诊断故意伤害同样重要,因为误诊儿童虐待可能对儿童及其家庭产生严重的医法后果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨结膜下出血(SCH)与呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)感染在婴儿中的潜在关联,因为SCH可能是非意外创伤(NAT)的警示征象。
设计与对象
对一名六周大的婴儿进行了病例报告,该婴儿患有结膜下出血和同时发生的RSV感染,并被评估是否存在非意外创伤。
核心方法
进行了全面的多学科评估,以检查是否存在创伤性损伤或虐待证据,包括临床检查和RSV感染的诊断测试。
主要结果
该婴儿患有结膜下出血和RSV感染,但未发现创伤性损伤或虐待证据。结膜下出血在一周内消退,患者在接受支持性治疗后无并发症康复。
解释与创新
作者认为,尽管炎症和压力相关的机制可能解释结膜下出血与RSV的关联,但这项观察性病例无法确立因果关系。该病例报告突出了一个尚未有文献记载的可能关联。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于扩大对RSV感染婴儿的结膜下出血的鉴别诊断,强调在考虑潜在故意伤害的同时,也要考虑医学原因的重要性。
局限与证据强度
由于研究的观察性质和缺乏对照组,该研究无法确立因果关系。由于这些限制,证据被认为是中等强度的。

Subconjunctival Hemorrhage and Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection in an Infant Evaluated for Non-accidental Trauma: A Case Report of a Possible Association

Authors: Kyle Hatley Koester, Kathryn Martin, Natalie C. Kerr, Lauren R. Burge
Original abstract (English)
A six-week-old previously healthy infant was found to have a subconjunctival hemorrhage (SCH) and concomitant respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection.Because SCH in an infant may represent a sentinel finding of non-accidental trauma (NAT), an extensive multidisciplinary evaluation was performed, which identified no traumatic injuries or evidence of abuse.The SCH resolved within one week, and the patient recovered with supportive care without complications.To our knowledge, SCH in association with RSV has not previously been reported, despite RSV being common in infants.As with SCH reported in association with other respiratory infections, inflammatory and pressure-related processes are possible mechanisms, although this observational case cannot establish causation.Reporting this possible association may broaden the differential diagnosis of SCH to include other medical causes in infants with RSV, while reinforcing the need for completing a thorough screening for abuse.Identifying medical causes for childhood injuries is as important as accurately diagnosing inflicted injury, as misdiagnosing child abuse can have serious medicolegal consequences for children and their families.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore the potential association between subconjunctival hemorrhage (SCH) and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection in an infant, as SCH may be a sentinel finding for non-accidental trauma (NAT).
Design and population
A case report was conducted on a six-week-old infant with a subconjunctival hemorrhage and concomitant RSV infection, evaluated for possible non-accidental trauma.
Core methods
An extensive multidisciplinary evaluation was performed to assess for traumatic injuries or evidence of abuse, including clinical examination and diagnostic testing for RSV infection.
Main results
The infant had a subconjunctival hemorrhage and RSV infection, but no traumatic injuries or evidence of abuse were found. The SCH resolved within one week, and the patient recovered with supportive care without complications.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest that while inflammatory and pressure-related processes may explain the association between SCH and RSV, this observational case cannot establish causation. This case report highlights a possible association that has not been previously documented.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the differential diagnosis of SCH in infants with RSV, emphasizing the importance of considering medical causes alongside potential inflicted injuries.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational nature and lack of control groups, which prevents establishing causation. The evidence is considered moderate due to these limitations.

一种纤毛上皮细胞系作为呼吸道合胞病毒研究的模型

A ciliated epithelial cell line as a model for respiratory syncytial virus research
作者： Sabine Blouquit-Laye, Gina Cosentino, Delphine Sitterlin, Marie-Anne Rameix-Welti, Sabine Blouquit‐Laye, Marie‐Anne Rameix‐Welti
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)是婴儿严重呼吸道感染的主要原因,并且在老年人中逐渐被认可为一种病原体。尽管纤毛细胞是RSV的主要靶标,但传统的RSV体外培养模型依赖于分化不良的细胞系,这些细胞系缺乏极化、紧密连接和纤毛等关键气道上皮特征。原代气道上皮培养更贴近体内环境,但获取较为困难。本研究评估了在气液界面(ALI)分化的人支气管上皮细胞系BCi-NS1.1作为RSV研究的替代模型。在ALI分化三周后,BCi-NS1.1培养物表现出高级分化特征,包括成熟的紧密连接、IL-13诱导的MUC5AC产生、约−20 mV的跨上皮电位差以及19%的纤毛细胞比例。这些培养物在纤毛上表达CX3CR1受体,这可能为RSV的入侵提供途径。RSV可以从顶端感染这些培养物,感染细胞数量在感染后第1天至第5天持续增加。感染后第2至第4天,顶端病毒滴度达到约2×10^5 PFU ml^{-1}。感染导致形成典型的病毒工厂,包括与包涵体相关的颗粒、顶端释放新的感染性病毒颗粒以及受感染细胞的受控脱落。由于脱落限制了亚细胞分析,我们生成了一种缺乏NS2蛋白(该蛋白促进细胞圆化和脱落)的RSV,以促进亚细胞分析。感染RSV-Delta NS2的细胞表现出较慢的脱落,并且在更长时间内保持其形态,从而能够观察到核糖核蛋白向顶端膜迁移的现象。总体而言,ALI分化的BCi-NS1.1细胞为研究RSV感染对上皮反应提供了合适的工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)是婴儿严重呼吸道感染的主要原因,并且在老年人中逐渐被认可为一种病原体。本研究旨在解决对更准确的RSV感染体外模型的需求。
设计与对象
本研究评估了在气液界面(ALI)分化的人支气管上皮细胞系BCi-NS1.1作为RSV研究的替代模型。该细胞系被用于模拟体内环境以研究RSV感染。
核心方法
研究人员在气液界面(ALI)对BCi-NS1.1细胞系进行了分化,并评估了其特性。他们还生成了一种缺乏NS2蛋白(该蛋白促进细胞圆化和脱落)的RSV,以促进亚细胞分析。
主要结果
在ALI分化三周后,BCi-NS1.1培养物表现出成熟的紧密连接、IL-13诱导的MUC5AC产生、约−20 mV的跨上皮电位差以及19%的纤毛细胞比例。这些培养物可以从顶端被RSV感染,感染细胞数量在感染后第1天至第5天持续增加。
解释与创新
作者认为,ALI分化的BCi-NS1.1细胞为研究RSV感染对上皮反应提供了合适的工具,因为它们密切模拟了体内环境,并支持RSV的复制和脱落。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,ALI分化的BCi-NS1.1细胞可用于研究RSV感染机制,这可能为未来关于诊断、治疗以及预防策略的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于体外观察,未考虑体内复杂性。然而,证据较强,因为它直接报告了RSV在相关细胞模型中的特性及其感染动态。

A ciliated epithelial cell line as a model for respiratory syncytial virus research

Authors: Sabine Blouquit-Laye, Gina Cosentino, Delphine Sitterlin, Marie-Anne Rameix-Welti, Sabine Blouquit‐Laye, Marie‐Anne Rameix‐Welti
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is the leading cause of severe respiratory infection in infants and an increasingly recognized pathogen in the elderly. Conventional in vitro culture models for RSV rely on poorly differentiated cell lines that lack key airway epithelial features such as polarization, tight junctions and cilia, despite ciliated cells being the main RSV target. Primary airway epithelial cultures better reflect in vivo conditions, but they are difficult to access. We evaluated the human bronchial epithelial cell line BCi-NS1.1 differentiated at an air-liquid interface (ALI) as an alternative model. Three weeks after ALI, BCi-NS1.1 cultures exhibited advanced differentiation, including mature tight junctions, IL-13-induced MUC5AC production, a transepithelial potential difference of around −20 mV and 19% ciliated cells. These cultures expressed CX3CR1 receptor in cilia, which may serve as an entry point for RSV. These cultures were readily infected by RSV from the apical side, with infected cell numbers increasing from day 1 to day 5 post-infection. Apical viral titres reached ~2×10 5 PFU ml −1 between day 2 and day 4. Infection led to the formation of typical viral factories with inclusion-body-associated granules, apical release of new infectious virions and controlled shedding of infected cells. Because shedding limits subcellular analysis, we generated an RSV lacking NS2, a protein promoting cell rounding and shedding. Cells infected with RSV-Delta NS2 exhibited slower shedding and maintained their shape longer, enabling visualization of ribonucleoprotein migration towards and across the apical membrane. Overall, ALI-differentiated BCi-NS1.1 cells provide a suitable tool for studying epithelial responses to RSV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is the leading cause of severe respiratory infection in infants and an increasingly recognized pathogen in the elderly. The study aimed to address the need for a more accurate in vitro model to study RSV infection.
Design and population
The study evaluated the human bronchial epithelial cell line BCi-NS1.1 differentiated at an air-liquid interface (ALI) as an alternative model for RSV research. The cell line was used to simulate in vivo conditions for studying RSV infection.
Core methods
The researchers differentiated the BCi-NS1.1 cell line at an air-liquid interface (ALI) and assessed its characteristics. They also generated an RSV lacking NS2, a protein promoting cell rounding and shedding, to facilitate subcellular analysis.
Main results
Three weeks after ALI differentiation, BCi-NS1.1 cultures exhibited mature tight junctions, IL-13-induced MUC5AC production, a transepithelial potential difference of around −20 mV and 19% ciliated cells. These cultures were readily infected by RSV from the apical side, with infected cell numbers increasing from day 1 to day 5 post-infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that ALI-differentiated BCi-NS1.1 cells provide a suitable tool for studying epithelial responses to RSV infection, as they closely mimic in vivo conditions and support RSV replication and shedding.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that BCi-NS1.1 cells differentiated at ALI can be used for studying RSV infection mechanisms, which may inform future research on diagnosis, treatment and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro observations and does not account for in vivo complexities. However, the evidence is strong as it directly reports on the characteristics and infection dynamics of RSV in a relevant cell model.

日本老年人严重呼吸道合胞病毒感染的健康与经济负担:一项回顾性行政数据库研究

Health and Economic Burden of Severe Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infections Among Older Adults in Japan: A Retrospective Administrative Database Study
作者： Shoko Akiyama, Yu-Fan Ho, Victor Preckler, Taizo Matsuki, Lauriane Harrington, Akiko Mizukami
摘要中文翻译目的:本回顾性队列研究研究调查了日本≥60岁成人中需要医疗干预的RSV感染的健康和经济负担。方法:数据从2008年9月1日至2021年8月31日收集自基于医院的医疗数据愿景(MDV)索赔数据库和基于保险的JMDC数据库。结果:2009年至2019年间共识别出178例病例,其中144例来自MDV数据库(平均年龄74岁),34例来自JMDC数据库(平均年龄64岁)。这些发现可能有助于制定针对严重RSV疾病的预防策略。| | 这些发现可能有助于制定RSV预防策略,包括疫苗接种。一项前瞻性研究估计,日本门诊老年人中RSV感染的发生率为2.4% [4]。我们使用来自两个行政索赔数据库的回顾性分析数据,探讨日本老年人中需要医疗干预的RSV感染的疾病和经济负担。2 材料与方法 2.1 研究设计 在这项回顾性观察性队列研究研究中,我们调查了2008年9月1日至2021年8月31日期间日本≥60岁老年人中RSV感染相关的健康和经济负担;差异通过配对样本t检验(p < 0.05)进行分析。RSV相关并发症在首次发作期间被记录;医疗资源利用(HCRU)和医疗费用在事件前(RSV事件前30天的平均费用,基于过去365天)和事件后(RSV事件后30天)期间进行了比较;差异通过配对样本t检验(p < 0.05)进行分析。RSV相关并发症在61.1%和55....
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估日本老年人严重呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)感染的健康和经济负担,因为由于缺乏常规检测和监测,该人群的RSV发病率和疾病负担可能被低估。
设计与对象
本回顾性队列研究研究使用了来自医疗数据愿景(MDV)医院基于的索赔数据库和基于保险的JMDC数据库的数据,时间范围为2008年9月1日至2021年8月31日。研究包括了178例RSV感染病例,其中144例来自MDV数据库,34例来自JMDC数据库,聚焦于≥60岁的成人。
核心方法
数据来自两个匿名的健康索赔数据库,使用配对样本t检验进行分析,统计学显著性设定为p < 0.05。比较了医疗资源利用(HCRU)和医疗费用在事件前和事件后期间的变化。
主要结果
在MDV 队列研究中,事件后30天内门诊就诊次数平均从1.21次增加到2.07次,住院次数从0.07次增加到0.37次,导致显著增加的医疗费用¥196,231(p < 0.001)。随着时间推移,影响逐渐减弱,在事件后365天期间未检测到统计学显著性。RSV病例数量在研究期间有所增加,分别达到2018–2019年的38例和2019–2020年的36例。
解释与创新
这些发现突显了RSV感染在老年人中的显著健康和经济负担,强调了需要针对性干预措施如疫苗接种,尤其是在日本老龄化人口的背景下。
科研与公卫意义
本研究为了解RSV感染在老年人中的医疗资源利用和费用提供了关键见解,支持针对监测、预防和管理策略的制定,特别是在这一易感人群中。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于其依赖行政数据库,可能缺乏详细的临床信息。由于研究的观察性质和潜在的未测量混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Health and Economic Burden of Severe Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infections Among Older Adults in Japan: A Retrospective Administrative Database Study

Authors: Shoko Akiyama, Yu-Fan Ho, Victor Preckler, Taizo Matsuki, Lauriane Harrington, Akiko Mizukami
Original abstract (English)
Objective This retrospective cohort study investigated the health and economic burden of medically attended RSV infections among adults ≥ 60 years in Japan. Methods Data were collected between 1 September 2008 and 31 August 2021 from the Medical Data Vision (MDV) hospital-based claims database and the insurance-based JMDC database. Results A total of 178 cases identified between 2009 and 2019 were included in the study, 144 were from the MDV database (mean age 74 years) and 34 from the JMDC database (mean age 64 years). These findings may help inform prevention strategies for severe RSV disease. | | These findings may help inform RSV prevention strategies, including vaccination. A prospective study estimated that RSV infection occurs in 2.4% of ambulatory older adults in Japan [4]. We conducted a retrospective analysis using data from two administrative claims databases to explore the disease and economic burden of medically attended RSV infections among older adults in Japan. 2 Materials and Methods 2.1 Study Design In this retrospective observational cohort study, we investigated the health and economic burden associated with RSV infection among older adults (≥ 60 years) in Japan from 1 September 2008 to 31 August 2021 (Supplementary Fig. Abstract Background Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute respiratory illness and severe outcomes in older adults. We investigated RSV-related complications during the first episode. Healthcare resource utilization (HCRU) and medical costs were compared between baseline (pre-event: 30-day average costs during the 365 days before RSV diagnosis) and post-event (30 days after RSV diagnosis); differences were explored using paired sample t-tests (p < 0.05). RSV-related complications were recorded in 61.1% and 55....
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the health and economic burden of severe respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infections in older adults in Japan, as the incidence and disease burden of RSV in this population is likely underestimated due to the lack of routine testing and surveillance.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted using data from the Medical Data Vision (MDV) hospital-based claims database and the insurance-based JMDC database, covering the period from 1 September 2008 to 31 August 2021. The study included 178 cases of RSV infection, with 144 from the MDV database and 34 from the JMDC database, focusing on adults aged ≥ 60 years.
Core methods
Data were collected from two anonymized health claims databases, and comparisons were analyzed using a paired sample t-test with statistical significance set at p < 0.05. Healthcare resource utilization (HCRU) and medical costs were compared between pre- and post-event periods.
Main results
In the MDV cohort, the average number of outpatient visits increased from 1.21 to 2.07, and hospitalizations increased from 0.07 to 0.37 within 30 days post-event, resulting in significant additional medical costs of ¥196,231 (p < 0.001). The impact decreased over time, with no statistical significance detected in the 365-day post-event period. The number of RSV cases increased over the study period, reaching 38 and 36 cases in 2018–2019 and 2019–2020, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the significant health and economic burden of RSV infections in older adults, emphasizing the need for targeted interventions such as vaccination, especially given Japan’s aging population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides critical insights into the healthcare resource utilization and costs associated with RSV infections in older adults, supporting the development of strategies for surveillance, prevention, and management in this vulnerable group.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on administrative databases, which may lack detailed clinical information. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and potential for unmeasured confounding factors.

白蛋白与碱性磷酸酶比率作为儿科 RSV 感染中细菌共感染表型的免疫代谢标志物:来自蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络的见解

Albumin-to-alkaline phosphatase ratio as an immuno-metabolic marker of bacterial co-infection phenotype in pediatric RSV infection: insights from protein–protein interaction networks
作者： Xiaoxiao Qu, Mengran Shi, Xingyu Lu, Enxin You, Jian Yu, Minghua Jiang, Ya Li, Qipeng Xie
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在调查 RSV 感染儿童中 AAPR 与革兰氏阳性 (G + ) 和革兰氏阴性 (G-) 细菌共感染表型之间的关联,评估其区分性能,并探索潜在的相关生物学途径。方法 我们回顾性分析了 153 例实验室确诊的 RSV 和细菌合并感染的儿童患者。结果 多变量logistic回归显示,AAPR升高与G+感染的可能性独立且呈负相关(OR = 0.37;95% CI:0.15-0.89(最高与伦理声明) 伦理批准和同意参与 该研究按照赫尔辛基宣言进行,并经温州医科大学附属第二医院和育英儿童医院伦理委员会批准(No. 给予回顾性研究)由于研究的性质和使用去识别化的临床数据,伦理委员会放弃了书面知情同意书的要求。图形摘要缩写 - AAPR: - 白蛋白与碱性磷酸酶比率 - ALB: - 白蛋白 - ALP: - 碱性磷酸酶 - ALT: - 丙氨酸转氨酶 - AUC: - 曲线下面积 - BP: - 生物过程 - CC: - 细胞成分 - CHD: - 先天性心脏病 - CI: - 置信区间 - CRP: - C反应蛋白 - G +: - 革兰氏阳性 - G-: - 革兰氏阴性 - GO: - 基因本体 - 中心基因: - PPI网络中高度连接的节点 - IQR: - 四分位距 - KEGG: - 京都基因百科全书和 基因组 - LPS: - 脂多糖 - MF: - 分子功能- NEU: - 中性粒细胞比例 - OR: - 优势比 - PLT: - 血小板计数 - PPI: - 蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用 - RCS: - 限制三次样条 - ROC: - 接收器操作 C...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
儿童 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染经常并发细菌合并感染,这会显着恶化临床结果。本研究旨在调查 RSV 感染儿童中 AAPR 与革兰氏阳性 (G + ) 和革兰氏阴性 (G-) 细菌共感染表型之间的关联,评估其区分性能,并探索潜在的相关生物学途径。
设计与对象
我们回顾性分析了 153 名实验室确诊的 RSV 和细菌合并感染的儿童患者。该研究按照赫尔辛基宣言进行,并获得温州医科大学第二附属医院和育英儿童医院伦理委员会批准。
核心方法
采用多变量逻辑回归和限制三次样条 (RCS) 分析来确定 AAPR 和感染表型之间的独立关联和剂量反应关系。使用受试者工作特征曲线下面积(AUC)对诊断性能进行量化。进行了探索性系统生物学分析,以确定与观察到的关联可能相关的候选基因和信号通路。
主要结果
多变量逻辑回归表明,升高的 AAPR 与 G+ 感染的可能性独立且呈负相关(OR = 0.37;95% CI:0.15-0.89,最高与 RCS 分析显示没有非线性关联的证据(非线性 P = 0.456)。单独的 AAPR 显示出适度的判别性能(AUC = 0.599)。将 AAPR 添加到 CRP、WBC、 NEU 将 AUC 从 0.455 增加到 0.613;然而,差异并未达到统计学显着性的常规阈值(P = 0.051)。
解释与创新
白蛋白与碱性磷酸酶比率(AAPR)最近成为一种新型免疫代谢标志物。探索性系统生物学分析确定了 IL-17、TNF 和其他炎症相关途径作为进一步研究的候选途径。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于开发免疫代谢标记物,以早期检测 RSV 感染儿童的细菌合并感染。这些见解可能有助于改进诊断策略和有针对性的治疗方法。
局限与证据强度
使用特定年龄的儿科 ALP 参考限进行标准化后,这种关联性减弱,并且 AUC 的差异未达到统计显着性的常规阈值。该研究是回顾性的,基于未识别的临床数据,这可能会引入偏差并限制普遍性。

Albumin-to-alkaline phosphatase ratio as an immuno-metabolic marker of bacterial co-infection phenotype in pediatric RSV infection: insights from protein–protein interaction networks

Authors: Xiaoxiao Qu, Mengran Shi, Xingyu Lu, Enxin You, Jian Yu, Minghua Jiang, Ya Li, Qipeng Xie
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to investigate the association between AAPR and Gram-positive (G + ) versus Gram-negative (G-) bacterial co-infection phenotypes among RSV-infected children, evaluate its discriminative performance, and explore potentially relevant biological pathways. Methods We retrospectively analyzed 153 pediatric patients with laboratory-confirmed RSV and bacterial co-infection. Results Multivariable logistic regression demonstrated that elevated AAPR was independently and inversely associated with the likelihood of G+ infection (OR = 0.37; 95% CI: 0.15–0.89 for the highest vs. Ethics declarations Ethics approval and consent to participate The study was conducted in accordance with the Declaration of Helsinki and was approved by the Ethics Committee of the Second Affiliated Hospital and Yuying Children’s Hospital of Wenzhou Medical University (No. Given the retrospective nature of the study and the use of de-identified clinical data, the requirement for written informed consent was waived by the Ethics Committee. Graphical abstract Abbreviations - AAPR: - Albumin-to-Alkaline Phosphatase Ratio - ALB: - Albumin - ALP: - Alkaline Phosphatase - ALT: - Alanine Aminotransferase - AUC: - Area Under the Curve - BP: - Biological Process - CC: - Cellular Component - CHD: - Congenital Heart Disease - CI: - Confidence Interval - CRP: - C-reactive Protein - G +: - Gram-positive - G-: - Gram-negative - GO: - Gene Ontology - Hub genes: - Highly connected nodes in a PPI network - IQR: - Interquartile Range - KEGG: - Kyoto Encyclopedia of Genes and Genomes - LPS: - Lipopolysaccharide - MF: - Molecular Function - NEU: - Neutrophil proportion - OR: - Odds Ratio - PLT: - Platelet count - PPI: - Protein-Protein Interaction - RCS: - Restricted Cubic Spline - ROC: - Receiver Operating C...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection in children is frequently complicated by bacterial co-infection, which significantly aggravates clinical outcomes. This study aimed to investigate the association between AAPR and Gram-positive (G + ) versus Gram-negative (G-) bacterial co-infection phenotypes among RSV-infected children, evaluate its discriminative performance, and explore potentially relevant biological pathways.
Design and population
We retrospectively analyzed 153 pediatric patients with laboratory-confirmed RSV and bacterial co-infection. The study was conducted in accordance with the Declaration of Helsinki and was approved by the Ethics Committee of the Second Affiliated Hospital and Yuying Children’s Hospital of Wenzhou Medical University.
Core methods
Multivariable logistic regression and restricted cubic spline (RCS) analyses were employed to determine the independent association and dose-response relationship between AAPR and infection phenotypes. Diagnostic performance was quantified using the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC). An exploratory systems biology analysis was performed to identify candidate genes and signaling pathways potentially relevant to the observed association.
Main results
Multivariable logistic regression demonstrated that elevated AAPR was independently and inversely associated with the likelihood of G+ infection (OR = 0.37; 95% CI: 0.15–0.89 for the highest vs. RCS analysis showed no evidence of a nonlinear association (P for non-linearity = 0.456). AAPR alone showed modest discriminative performance (AUC = 0.599). Addition of AAPR to CRP, WBC, and NEU increased the AUC from 0.455 to 0.613; however, the difference did not reach the conventional threshold for statistical significance (P = 0.051).
Interpretation and novelty
The Albumin-to-Alkaline Phosphatase Ratio (AAPR) has recently emerged as a novel immuno-metabolic marker. Exploratory systems biology analysis identified IL-17-, TNF-, and other inflammation-related pathways as candidates for further investigation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of immuno-metabolic markers for early detection of bacterial co-infection in RSV-infected children. These insights could contribute to improved diagnostic strategies and targeted therapeutic approaches.
Limitations and evidence strength
The association was attenuated after normalization using age-specific pediatric ALP reference limits, and the difference in AUC did not reach the conventional threshold for statistical significance. The study is retrospective and based on de-identified clinical data, which may introduce bias and limit generalizability.
